<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">🥳 🚀 What's New <a href="#-table-of-contents">🔝</a></h2><a id="user-content---whats-new-" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 🥳 🚀 What's New 🔝" href="#--whats-new-"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>The default branch has been switched to <code>main</code>(previous <code>1.x</code>) from <code>master</code>(current <code>0.x</code>), and we encourage users to migrate to the latest version with more supported models, stronger pre-training checkpoints and simpler coding. Please refer to <a href="https://mmaction2.readthedocs.io/en/latest/migration.html" rel="nofollow">Migration Guide</a> for more details.</strong></p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Release (2023.10.12)</strong>: v1.2.0 with the following new features:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Support VindLU multi-modality algorithm and the Training of ActionClip</li>
<li>Support lightweight model MobileOne TSN/TSM</li>
<li>Support video retrieval dataset MSVD</li>
<li>Support SlowOnly K700 feature to train localization models</li>
<li>Support Video and Audio Demos</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">📖 Introduction <a href="#-table-of-contents">🔝</a></h2><a id="user-content--introduction-" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 📖 Introduction 🔝" href="#-introduction-"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">MMAction2 is an open-source toolbox for video understanding based on PyTorch.
It is a part of the <a href="http://openmmlab.com/" rel="nofollow">OpenMMLab</a> project.</p>
<div align="center" dir="auto">
  <a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" href="https://github.com/open-mmlab/mmaction2/raw/main/resources/mmaction2_overview.gif"><img src="https://github.com/open-mmlab/mmaction2/raw/main/resources/mmaction2_overview.gif" width="380px" data-animated-image="" style="max-width: 100%;"></a>
  <a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer nofollow" href="https://user-images.githubusercontent.com/34324155/123989146-2ecae680-d9fb-11eb-916b-b9db5563a9e5.gif"><img src="https://user-images.githubusercontent.com/34324155/123989146-2ecae680-d9fb-11eb-916b-b9db5563a9e5.gif" width="380px" data-animated-image="" style="max-width: 100%;"></a>
  <p dir="auto"> Action Recognition on Kinetics-400 (left) and Skeleton-based Action Recognition on NTU-RGB+D-120 (right)</p>
</div>
<div align="center" dir="auto">
  <a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer nofollow" href="https://user-images.githubusercontent.com/30782254/155710881-bb26863e-fcb4-458e-b0c4-33cd79f96901.gif"><img src="https://user-images.githubusercontent.com/30782254/155710881-bb26863e-fcb4-458e-b0c4-33cd79f96901.gif" width="580px" data-animated-image="" style="max-width: 100%;"></a><br>
    <p dir="auto">Skeleton-based Spatio-Temporal Action Detection and Action Recognition Results on Kinetics-400</p>
</div>
<div align="center" dir="auto">
  <a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" href="https://github.com/open-mmlab/mmaction2/raw/main/resources/spatio-temporal-det.gif"><img src="https://github.com/open-mmlab/mmaction2/raw/main/resources/spatio-temporal-det.gif" width="800px" data-animated-image="" style="max-width: 100%;"></a><br>
    <p dir="auto">Spatio-Temporal Action Detection Results on AVA-2.1</p>
</div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">🎁 Major Features <a href="#-table-of-contents">🔝</a></h2><a id="user-content--major-features-" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 🎁 Major Features 🔝" href="#-major-features-"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Modular design</strong>: We decompose a video understanding framework into different components. One can easily construct a customized video understanding framework by combining different modules.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Support five major video understanding tasks</strong>: MMAction2 implements various algorithms for multiple video understanding tasks, including action recognition, action localization, spatio-temporal action detection, skeleton-based action detection and video retrieval.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Well tested and documented</strong>: We provide detailed documentation and API reference, as well as unit tests.</p>
</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">🛠️ Installation <a href="#-table-of-contents">🔝</a></h2><a id="user-content-️-installation-" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 🛠️ Installation 🔝" href="#️-installation-"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">MMAction2 depends on <a href="https://pytorch.org/" rel="nofollow">PyTorch</a>, <a href="https://github.com/open-mmlab/mmcv">MMCV</a>, <a href="https://github.com/open-mmlab/mmengine">MMEngine</a>, <a href="https://github.com/open-mmlab/mmdetection">MMDetection</a> (optional) and <a href="https://github.com/open-mmlab/mmpose">MMPose</a> (optional).</p>
<p dir="auto">Please refer to <a href="https://mmaction2.readthedocs.io/en/latest/get_started/installation.html" rel="nofollow">install.md</a> for detailed instructions.</p>
<details>
<summary>Quick instructions</summary>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="conda create --name openmmlab python=3.8 -y
conda activate openmmlab
conda install pytorch torchvision -c pytorch  # This command will automatically install the latest version PyTorch and cudatoolkit, please check whether they match your environment.
pip install -U openmim
mim install mmengine
mim install mmcv
mim install mmdet  # optional
mim install mmpose  # optional
git clone https://github.com/open-mmlab/mmaction2.git
cd mmaction2
pip install -v -e ."><pre>conda create --name openmmlab python=3.8 -y
conda activate openmmlab
conda install pytorch torchvision -c pytorch  <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> This command will automatically install the latest version PyTorch and cudatoolkit, please check whether they match your environment.</span>
pip install -U openmim
mim install mmengine
mim install mmcv
mim install mmdet  <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> optional</span>
mim install mmpose  <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> optional</span>
git clone https://github.com/open-mmlab/mmaction2.git
<span class="pl-c1">cd</span> mmaction2
pip install -v -e <span class="pl-c1">.</span></pre></div>
</details>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">👀 Model Zoo <a href="#-table-of-contents">🔝</a></h2><a id="user-content--model-zoo-" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 👀 Model Zoo 🔝" href="#-model-zoo-"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">🤝 Acknowledgement <a href="#-table-of-contents">🔝</a></h2><a id="user-content--acknowledgement-" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 🤝 Acknowledgement 🔝" href="#-acknowledgement-"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">MMAction2 is an open-source project that is contributed by researchers and engineers from various colleges and companies.
We appreciate all the contributors who implement their methods or add new features and users who give valuable feedback.
We wish that the toolbox and benchmark could serve the growing research community by providing a flexible toolkit to reimplement existing methods and develop their new models.</p>
